Implementations should raise on failure;\n    ``run_eval`` classifies any non-``KeyboardInterrupt`` /\n    ``SystemExit`` exception as ``ERRORED``.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def spawn(self, ctx: ExecutorContext) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - protocol\n        ...\n\n    def inject(self, ctx: ExecutorContext) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - protocol\n        ...\n\n    def observe(self, ctx: ExecutorContext) -> ObservedRun:  # pragma: no cover - protocol\n        ...\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Internal sentinel for \"no observed run yet\"\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n_NO_OBSERVED_RUN = ObservedRun(\n    exit_code=-1,\n    stdout=\"\",\n    stderr=\"\",\n    duration_sec=0.0,\n    jsonl_paths=MappingProxyType({}),\n    artifacts=MappingProxyType({}),\n)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Public entrypoint\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef run_eval(\n    spec: EvalSpec,\n    *,\n    home: HomeIsolation,\n    config: EvalConfig,\n    run_dir: Path,\n    artifacts_dir: Path,\n    stdout_path: Path,\n    stderr_path: Path,\n    transcript_path: Path,\n    executor: LifecycleExecutor,\n    expect_callables: Sequence[ExpectCallable] = (),\n    deploy_hooks: Callable[[Path], None] = deploy_hooks_to,\n    on_teardown_error: Optional[Callable[[BaseException], None]] = None,\n    keep_home_on_pass: bool = False,\n) -> EvalOutcome:\n    \"\"\"Execute the FR-LC1 7-step lifecycle and return an ``EvalOutcome``.\n\n    The function is the *skeleton* on top of which COMP-004 EvalRunner\n    (T03.05) layers per-eval JSONL logging and per-eval timeout\n    enforcement. See ``artifacts/D-0048/spec.md`` for the full lifecycle\n    + status-mapping contract.\n\n    Arguments are keyword-only (after ``spec``) so future field\n    additions cannot silently re-bind positional callers. The default\n    ``deploy_hooks`` resolves to the COMP-014 ``deploy_hooks_to``\n    function; tests inject a stub.\n\n    Raises\n    ------\n    KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit\n        Re-raised verbatim after running ``home.teardown(keep=True)`` so\n        NFR-REL1 (T03.07) can convert them into an ``INTERRUPTED``\n        outcome at the orchestrator layer. Every other exception is\n        captured and folded into an ``EvalOutcome`` with status\n        ``ERRORED``.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Per-step exception capture lives in ``_LifecycleState`` so the\n    # status-mapping helper can read every step's result without\n    # threading positional state through the function body.\n    state = _LifecycleState(spec=spec)\n\n    try:\n        # Step 1 — build isolation. ``HomeIsolation.setup`` is itself\n        # idempotency-guarded; the atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) preserves\n        # the partial HOME on failure so ``teardown(keep=True)`` keeps\n        # forensic artifacts on disk.\n        try:\n            home.setup(config=config)\n        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):\n            raise\n        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — captured for status mapping\n            state.record_harness_failure(step=\"setup\", exc=exc)\n            return _finalize(\n                state=state,\n                home=home,\n                on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error,\n                keep_home_on_pass=keep_home_on_pass,\n            )\n\n        home_path = home.home_path\n\n        # Step 2 — deploy hooks. Injectable so tests stub the deploy\n        # without a real ``hooks.json`` on disk.\n        try:\n            deploy_hooks(home_path)\n        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):\n            raise\n        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — captured for status mapping\n            state.record_harness_failure(step=\"deploy_hooks\", exc=exc)\n            return _finalize(\n                state=state,\n                home=home,\n                on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error,\n                keep_home_on_pass=keep_home_on_pass,\n            )\n\n        # Build the executor's pre-observe view. ``env`` is taken from\n        # ``HomeIsolation.env()`` so the executor sees the canonical\n        # HOME / CLAUDE_SESSION_ID overlay.\n        exec_ctx = ExecutorContext(\n            eval_spec=spec,\n            home=home,\n            home_path=home_path,\n            run_dir=run_dir,\n            artifacts_dir=artifacts_dir,\n            stdout_path=stdout_path,\n            stderr_path=stderr_path,\n            transcript_path=transcript_path,\n            env=MappingProxyType(dict(home.env())),\n        )\n\n        # Step 3 — spawn.\n        try:\n            executor.spawn(exec_ctx)\n        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):\n            raise\n        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — captured for status mapping\n            state.record_harness_failure(step=\"spawn\", exc=exc)\n            return _finalize(\n                state=state,\n                home=home,\n                on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error,\n                keep_home_on_pass=keep_home_on_pass,\n            )\n\n        # Step 4 — inject.\n        try:\n            executor.inject(exec_ctx)\n        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):\n            raise\n        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — captured for status mapping\n            state.record_harness_failure(step=\"inject\", exc=exc)\n            return _finalize(\n                state=state,\n                home=home,\n                on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error,\n                keep_home_on_pass=keep_home_on_pass,\n            )\n\n        # Step 5 — observe.\n        try:\n            observed = executor.observe(exec_ctx)\n        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):\n            raise\n        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — captured for status mapping\n            state.record_harness_failure(step=\"observe\", exc=exc)\n            return _finalize(\n                state=state,\n                home=home,\n                on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error,\n                keep_home_on_pass=keep_home_on_pass,\n            )\n        state.record_observed(observed)\n\n        # Step 6 — assert. Build the per-Expect EvalContext now that we\n        # have stdout/stderr/exit_code/duration, then apply each\n        # callable in declaration order. A callable that raises folds\n        # into ERRORED with a partial ``expects`` tuple (whatever\n        # callables ran before the raise).\n        ctx = EvalContext.from_runner_state(\n            eval_spec=spec,\n            home=home,\n            run_dir=run_dir,\n            artifacts_dir=artifacts_dir,\n            stdout_path=stdout_path,\n            stderr_path=stderr_path,\n            transcript_path=transcript_path,\n            jsonl_paths=observed.jsonl_paths,\n            env=exec_ctx.env,\n            exit_code=observed.exit_code,\n            stdout=observed.stdout,\n            stderr=observed.stderr,\n            duration_sec=observed.duration_sec,\n            artifacts=observed.artifacts,\n        )\n\n        for callable_ in expect_callables:\n            try:\n                result = callable_(ctx)\n            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):\n                raise\n            except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n                state.record_harness_failure(step=\"assert\", exc=exc)\n                return _finalize(\n                    state=state,\n                    home=home,\n                    on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error,\n                    keep_home_on_pass=keep_home_on_pass,\n                )\n            state.record_expect(result)\n\n        return _finalize(\n            state=state,\n            home=home,\n            on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error,\n            keep_home_on_pass=keep_home_on_pass,\n        )\n\n    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):\n        # NFR-REL1 contract: cooperative cancellation. Teardown the\n        # partial HOME with ``keep=True`` so forensics survive, then\n        # re-raise so the orchestrator's signal handler can convert this\n        # into an ``INTERRUPTED`` outcome at the suite layer.\n        _safe_teardown(home, keep=True, on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error)\n        raise\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Internal state + classification helpers\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _LifecycleState:\n    \"\"\"Mutable per-call state captured by ``run_eval``.\n\n    Kept private to the module — the public surface is ``EvalOutcome``.\n    The dataclass groups everything the classification helper needs so\n    the body of ``run_eval`` stays close to the seven-step structure\n    without smuggling positional results through ``return`` tuples.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    spec: EvalSpec\n    observed: ObservedRun = record_observed(self, observed: ObservedRun) -> None:\n        self.observed = observed\n\n    def record_expect(self, result: ExpectResult) -> None:\n        self.expects.append(result)\n\n    def _LifecycleState) -> tuple[EvalStatus, Optional[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Return ``(status, error_class)`` for the captured lifecycle state.\n\n    Encodes the status-mapping rules from ``artifacts/D-0048/spec.md``:\n\n    * Any captured harness exception -> ``ERRORED`` with the exception's\n      fully-qualified classname.\n    * Otherwise, all ``ExpectResult.passed == True`` -> ``PASS``.\n    * Otherwise -> ``FAIL``.\n\n    An empty ``expects`` tuple with no harness failure is ``PASS`` by\n    design (the eval ran cleanly and the manifest asked nothing of it).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    if state.harness_failure_exc is not None:\n        exc = state.harness_failure_exc\n        cls = type(exc)\n        return \"ERRORED\", f\"{cls.__module__}.{cls.__qualname__}\"\n\n    if all(result.passed for result in state.expects):\n        return \"PASS\", None\n\n    return \"FAIL\", None\n\n\ndef _finalize(\n    *,\n    state: _LifecycleState,\n    home: HomeIsolation,\n their\n    HOME for forensic inspection. Teardown failures are routed through\n    ``on_teardown_error`` so they cannot flip a PASS into a FAIL — the\n    design-spec invariant is that teardown is best-effort.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    status, error_class = _classify_outcome(state)\n\n    keep = True if status != \"PASS\" else keep_home_on_pass\n    _safe_teardown(home, keep=keep, on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error)\n\n    # ``duration_sec`` is ``0.0`` until observe completed. return an ``EvalOutcome``.","+","+        Wraps :func:`run_eval` with logging proxies + a per-eval timeout","+        budget. The lifecycle itself is unchanged — every step still","+        runs through the FR-LC1 skeleton — so the status mapping rules","+        from ``D-0048/spec.md`` apply verbatim except for the TIMEOUT","+        branch this class adds.","+        \"\"\"","+","+        log = _JsonlLog(eval_id=spec.id, clock=self._clock)","+        timeout_sec = self._resolve_timeout(spec)","+","+        # Holders for the worker thread. ``outcome_holder`` is populated","+        # on clean return; ``exc_holder`` is populated when run_eval","+        # raised (KeyboardInterrupt / SystemExit, mainly — every other","+        # exception is folded into an ERRORED outcome by run_eval","+        # itself).","+        outcome_holder: dict[str, EvalOutcome] = {}","+        exc_holder: dict[str, BaseException] = {}","+","+        logged_home = _LoggingHomeProxy(self._home, log)","+        logged_executor = _LoggingExecutor(self._executor, log)","+        wrapped_expects = tuple(","+            _wrap_expect_with_log(c, index=i, log=log)","+            for i, c in enumerate(self._expect_callables)","+        )","+","+        # The wrapped ``deploy_hooks`` emits ``hooks_deployed`` once the","+        # underlying call returns; the spec does not require this event","+        # but it is the natural place to record that step 2 completed.","+        original_deploy = self._deploy_hooks","+","+        def logged_deploy(home_path: Path) -> None:","+            original_deploy(home_path)","+            log.emit(EvalRunner.EVENT_HOOKS_DEPLOYED, step=\"deploy_hooks\")","+","+        # ``on_teardown_error`` is invoked by ``_safe_teardown`` when","+        # teardown raises. The proxy already logs the failure via","+        # ``EVENT_TEARDOWN_ERROR``; the callback is a no-op so the","+        # caller-injected behaviour (preserved by FR-LC1) keeps working","+        # if a future caller wires one through ``EvalRunner``.","+        def on_teardown_error(_exc: BaseException) -> None:","+            return None","+","+        def worker() -> None:","+            try:","+                outcome_holder[\"result\"] = run_eval(","+                    spec,","+                    home=logged_home,  # type: ignore[arg-type]  # duck-typed proxy","+                    config=self._config,","+                    run_dir=self._run_dir,","+                    artifacts_dir=self._artifacts_dir,","+                    stdout_path=self._stdout_path,","+                    stderr_path=self._stderr_path,","+                    transcript_path=self._transcript_path,","+                    executor=logged_executor,  # type: ignore[arg-type]  # duck-typed proxy","+                    expect_callables=wrapped_expects,","+                    deploy_hooks=logged_deploy,","+                    on_teardown_error=on_teardown_error,","+                    keep_home_on_pass=self._keep_home_on_pass,","+                )","+            except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — captured for re-raise","+                exc_holder[\"exc\"] = exc","+","+        # ``daemon=True`` so a hung worker cannot block process exit.","+        # NFR-REL1 (T03.07) wires the PtyDriver kill + zombie reap that","+        # makes a hung worker rare in production.","+        thread = threading.Thread(","+            target=worker,","+            name=f\"EvalRunner[{spec.id}]\",","+            daemon=True,","+        )","+        thread.start()","+        thread.join(timeout=timeout_sec)","+","+        if thread.is_alive():","+            return self._handle_timeout(spec=spec, log=log, timeout_sec=timeout_sec)","+","+        # Worker returned. Either run_eval produced an outcome or raised","+        # an interrupt; the latter must propagate to the orchestrator so","+        # NFR-REL1 can mark INTERRUPTED. Flush the JSONL log first so","+        # the partial events survive the re-raise.","+        if \"exc\" in exc_holder:","+            exc = exc_holder[\"exc\"]","+            self._flush_log(log)","+            raise exc","+","+        outcome = outcome_holder[\"result\"]","+        # Record the terminal classification so post-mortem tooling can","+        # tell whether the run ended cleanly or was reclassified.","+        log.emit(","+            EvalRunner.EVENT_OUTCOME,","+            step=\"outcome\",","+            extra={","+                \"status\": outcome.status,","+                \"duration_sec\": outcome.duration_sec,","+                \"error_class\": outcome.error_class,","+            },","+        )","+        self._flush_log(log)","+        return outcome","+","+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------","+    # Internals","+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------","+","+    def _resolve_timeout(self, spec: EvalSpec) -> Optional[float]:","+        \"\"\"Return the resolved per-eval timeout, or ``None`` for unbounded.","+","+        ``spec.timeout_sec`` takes precedence; if absent or 0 the","+        runner falls back to ``default_timeout_sec``. ``0`` is treated","+        as \"use the default\" rather than \"no timeout\" because a literal","+        zero would always fire and is never useful in practice.","+        \"\"\"","+        spec_timeout = spec.timeout_sec","+        if spec_timeout is not None and spec_timeout > 0:","+            return float(spec_timeout)","+        if self._default_timeout_sec is not None and self._default_timeout_sec > 0:","+            return float(self._default_timeout_sec)","+        return None","+","+    def _handle_timeout(","+        self,","+        *,","+        spec: EvalSpec,","+        log: _JsonlLog,","+        timeout_sec: Optional[float],","+    ) -> EvalOutcome:","+        \"\"\"Emit timeout events, attempt best-effort teardown, return outcome.\"\"\"","+        budget = float(timeout_sec) if timeout_sec is not None else 0.0","+        log.emit(","+            EvalRunner.EVENT_TIMEOUT_FIRED,","+            step=\"timeout\",","+            extra={\"timeout_sec\": budget},","+        )","+        log.emit(","+            EvalRunner.EVENT_TEARDOWN_STARTED,","+            step=\"teardown\",","+            extra={\"keep\": True},","+        )","+        try:","+            # The worker may still hold references to the HomeIsolation","+            # instance; HomeIsolation.teardown is idempotent enough that","+            # a main-thread call is safe even if the worker is mid-step.","+            # Failures are swallowed so the TIMEOUT outcome always","+            # returns cleanly.","+            if getattr(self._home, \"is_set_up\", False):","+                self._home.teardown(keep=True)","+        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — best-effort","+            cls = type(exc)","+            log.emit(","+                EvalRunner.EVENT_TEARDOWN_ERROR,","+                step=\"teardown\",","+                extra={","+                    \"error_class\": f\"{cls.__module__}.{cls.__qualname__}\",","+                    \"message\": str(exc),","+                },","+            )","+","+        outcome = EvalOutcome(","+            eval_id=spec.id,","+            title=spec.title,","+            status=\"TIMEOUT\",","+            duration_sec=budget,","+            expects=(),","+            skip_reason=None,","+            skip_flag_triggered=None,","+            artifacts={},","+            error_class=\"builtins.TimeoutError\",","+        )","+        log.emit(","+            EvalRunner.EVENT_OUTCOME,","+            step=\"outcome\",","+            extra={","+                \"status\": outcome.status,","+                \"duration_sec\": outcome.duration_sec,","+                \"error_class\": outcome.error_class,","+            },","+        )","+        self._flush_log(log)","+        return outcome","+","+    def _flush_log(self, log: _JsonlLog) -> None:","+        \"\"\"Write the buffered JSONL log under ``home_path/.eval-logs/``.","+","+        Best-effort: if ``home_path`` is not yet resolvable (setup","+        failed before ``home_path`` was assigned) the events are","+        discarded. The JSONL log is a forensic artifact, never","+        load-bearing for outcome classification.","+        \"\"\"","+        home_path = self._resolve_home_path()","+        if home_path is None:","+            return","+        # ``eval_id`` is regex-guarded by FR-SCH2 upstream so it is safe","+        # to use verbatim in a filename. We still take the first event","+        # for the id to avoid trusting any later mutation.","+        snapshot = log.snapshot()","+        if not snapshot:","+            return","+        eval_id = snapshot[0].eval_id","+        log_path = home_path / self.LOG_DIR_RELPATH / f\"{eval_id}.jsonl\"","+        log.write_to(log_path)","+","+    def _resolve_home_path(self) -> Optional[Path]:","+        \"\"\"Return the per-eval HOME path if available, ``None`` otherwise.","+","+        ``HomeIsolation.home_path`` raises when setup has not run; we","+        catch that and return ``None`` so a setup-time failure never","+        promotes itself into a runner exception.","+        \"\"\"","+        try:","+            return Path(self._home.home_path)","+        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — home_path may raise pre-setup","+            return None"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
